UIOpeningText.uitext,"As the owner of a new cleaning business, your friend was excited when he got the call. The old Smith estate has a large, rambling house that was abandoned years ago. Your buddy asked you to fill in for him today, so you are here with friends, ready to work. You were excited for the opportunity to see the inside of the house--and the pay is good--but now that you are here, looking at the house, you wonder if you made the right decision.\n\nYou walk up the long road, slowly passing the front of the now-dilapidated mansion. The yards are dry and withered, cobblestones are upended, broken, and half-buried, and dead trees are strewn across the property. You cannot see through the windows for all the mud and spider webs. Looking around the yard, you remember something you were told about three kids found dead here 10 years ago. \n\nAfter the murders, old Mr Smith was never seen again. Folks had laughed off rumors that the kids never really left, but no buyer was found. Now they want to try to sell it again. You are here to get the house ready. And the estate looks like it has not been tended to since Mr Smith disappeared. \n\nAs you round the corner of the house, you come to a driveway and what looks like a gate at the end. "

UIGameOverLoseRescueBoy.uitext,|||A boy’s scream cuts the night and fades into complete silence. Another scream and you can feel a power welling beneath your feet. The gate is opening and you were unable to stop it. You run for the doors as the house rumbles and the floor buckles. You aren’t sure what will happen next, but you know you failed.\n\nSarah screams, then yells over the noise, "We failed and I can feel the boy’s pain like it was mine 10 years ago. You must get out before it is too late. I cannot stay and cannot leave--I am trapped." She disappears and you briefly wonder what will happen to her.\n\nYou run from the house and don’t look back. The farther you get, the louder the screaming becomes and you realize it is in your head. Somehow, you are connected to what happened and you wonder what that means for you. You are momentarily relieved when it stops, but the roar that replaces it makes you realize something worse is coming.\n\nYou and your companions keep running, but you know you can never get far enough away.\n\nThe End.|||
UIGameOverWinStopCultistsText.uitext,|||As the cultist falls to the floor you are satisfied that there won’t be enough sacrifice to keep the gate open. It would be best if you got away from this house and town as quickly as possible.\n\nSarah leans in to whisper, "You have done well. The ritual cannot be performed today. It will be years before another time comes and I will wait here to be ready for it. Thank you." She reaches out to hug you but as her arms pass through you, she fades and is gone.\n\nYour friend will not be happy about the job, and less happy when you tell him he should run too. But you know that the alternative would have been worse. You run from the house smiling in the face of uncertainty because, today at least, you won.\n\nThe End.|||

UIGameOverLoseStopCultistsText.uitext,|||Somewhere in the house, a low rumble begins, almost imperceptible at first as it grows and develops. They will bring their god to destroy the world."\n\nThe remaining cultists turn and run deeper into the house. UIGameOverWinRescueBoyText.uitext,|||Looking through the gate, you see no one. You grab the boy and run down the driveway, heading back into town. But there will be time to think when you get away from this house.\n\nSarah whispers to you, "Thank you for stopping them. It makes me happy that the boy will not suffer my fate. UIGameOverLoseStopRitualText.uitext,|||Everything goes silent for a moment, then the house shudders and the floor rises under your feet with a deafening crack. Looking around desperately for a way to escape, you find yourself trapped as the house collapses around you. A giant shadow rises up from rubble and looks around--the gate is open and the god has arrived.\n\nSarah desperately whispers, "It could not be worse. We failed in the worst possible way. With that monster here, I can no longer stay in this world, but I have nowhere else to go. I love you, Brother, but I fear our failure will leave us wandering the cosmos, alone and without a home."\n\nYou begin choking and gagging on smoke and dust. The shadow falling across your face mirrors the shadow across your heart. You failed and the world will pay the price. The shadow reaches down for you and everything goes black. It is over.\n\nThe end.|||
UIGameOverHalfWinStopRitualText.uitext,|||The cultists kill the last of your companions as the gate sputters and dims. You have a moment to reflect on what happened.
